All of that really only shows up during these same bad stretches—when I'm feeling really low, guilty, and just worn down. When I'm actually doing okay, or back when I was doing okay, I didn't experience any of that.\n\nDoctor: Have you had more than one period in your life where you felt this heavy sadness?\n\nPatient: No, this is... this is really the first time. Before this whole stretch started, I was always pretty steady. I've never gone through a period like this before where I felt this down.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods of significant improvement in your mood since this heavy sadness started, where you felt back to your normal self for a while?\n\nPatient: No, there really hasn't been any break in it. It's just been a constant thing... for about a year and two months now. It all started when a pipe burst at my old place and I had to move into this much smaller apartment. All my normal routines just kind of fell apart after that, and I haven't felt like my normal self since.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you've lost interest in activities you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I really have. Most days I just wake up with this flat, heavy sadness. It's like... the things that used to reset me, you know? Putting on a record, cooking a decent meal, tinkering with electronics... they just don't reach me anymore. I don't really have the drive to do any of it.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any other episodes of depression like this one before in your life?\n\nPatient: No, like I mentioned earlier, this is really the first time. I've never gone through anything like this before.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least two months where you didn't feel this heavy sadness?\n\nPatient: No, I really haven't. Like I said, it's just been constant since the move... so for over a year now, there hasn't been a break like that at all. Just the same heavy feeling every day.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any earlier episodes of depression that lasted for less than two months before this current one started?\n\nPatient: No, no earlier episodes. This whole thing just started after the move, and I've never felt anything like this before then. So, no short episodes or anything like that in the past.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had an episode of depression that lasted for at least two weeks before this current one started?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. Like I was saying, this is the first time I've ever dealt with anything like this. Before the move, I was fine. There weren't any two-week periods of feeling like this, or anything close to it, before all this started.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where you felt completely back to normal for at least two months since this depression started?\n\nPatient: No, definitely not. It's just been a steady downward slide since we relocated. I haven't had a single day where I felt like my old self, let alone two whole months. It's just been this constant weight the entire time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had an episode of depression that lasted less than two weeks before this current one started?\n\nPatient: No, not even for a few days. I... I really haven't had any periods of feeling like this before we relocated. This is the very first time I've ever dealt with this, regardless of how short or long it lasted. So, no, nothing at all before this current situation started.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any episodes of depression before this one that met the same criteria as your current feelings?\n\nPatient: No, I really haven't. Like I've been saying, this whole experience is completely new to me. I've never felt this way before, not even once, until we moved. There haven't been any other episodes at all.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any episodes of depression before this one that lasted at least two weeks?\n\nPatient: No, nothing like that. I know we're kind of covering the same ground, but... yeah, I've never had any period of depression that lasted two weeks before this. Or any amount of time, really. It's just been this one time, ever since the move.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any episodes of depression before this one that were separated from this one by a period of at least two months where you felt completely back to normal?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. Like I mentioned, I haven't had any depression before this current episode at all. So there wasn't anything to recover from or have a normal period after. This is really the only time I've gone through this.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"
